# Functional audit source register

Baseline `2a9380abf97a89ec35d91f7c7a9b03327364634f`. This is the closure/discovery index supporting the manually traced functional inventories. A declaration/reference is **not** proof of execution or unused code. Conservative unresolved reachability means retain, not delete. Source occurrences in [control register](FUNCTIONAL_AUDIT_CONTROL_REGISTER.md) identify callbacks/XML control sites; their domain cross-references identify the shared data/effect chain.

## Baseline source and composed dependency provenance

App source at the baseline; build requires sibling MediaLib and FileCoreLibrary. Pinned dependency source is read-only outside this repository. MediaLib `5758074049bc153ac4967b4ecd21886469270076`, FileCoreLibrary `c4b760c55102c72d45f68b0d16999fc1f4f013a7`, AVOS `2cf21c486c7bf244c49b78d2220197a6d75396dd` from [.github/build/nova-ci.xml](https://github.com/heretoecode/Supernova/blob/2a9380abf97a89ec35d91f7c7a9b03327364634f/.github/build/nova-ci.xml). Repository prepare/patch scripts overlay pinned libraries/native code in a build. No overlays were applied to tracked sources; no APK/build/restore was run. Pinned base links must be read together with relevant patch links; runtime delivery remains the historical candidate evidence, not this audit.

## Manifest declarations and permissions

`exported` omitted is recorded as omitted, not assumed false: target/API merger rules can alter it. Activity aliases and intent filters are independent reachability; class-name/reflection/private Java-reference searches alone cannot prove dead. Services/receivers may start by Android/job/broadcast/provider initialization without a visible rail item. Root and both app variants are included; dependency manifests are included as merge inputs (not all necessarily final APK).
